import MethodEndpoint from "@theme/ApiExplorer/MethodEndpoint";
import ParamsDetails from "@theme/ParamsDetails";
import RequestSchema from "@theme/RequestSchema";
import StatusCodes from "@theme/StatusCodes";
import OperationTabs from "@theme/OperationTabs";
import TabItem from "@theme/TabItem";
import Heading from "@theme/Heading";

<Heading
  as={"h1"}
  className={"openapi__heading"}
  children={"创建自动化"}
>
</Heading>

<MethodEndpoint
  method={"post"}
  path={"/automations"}
  context={"endpoint"}
>
  
</MethodEndpoint>



创建一个 Flow 自动化。自动化由事件或定时触发；config 必填，name 可回退到 config.metadata.name。创建成功后返回一个 automationId。

<Heading
  id={"request"}
  as={"h2"}
  className={"openapi-tabs__heading"}
  children={"Request"}
>
</Heading>

<ParamsDetails
  parameters={[{"name":"Lang","in":"header","required":false,"schema":{"type":"string","example":"{{Lang}}"},"description":"语言。使用 `zh-CN` 表示中文，使用 `en-US` 表示英文。"}]}
>
  
</ParamsDetails>

<RequestSchema
  title={"Body"}
  body={{"required":true,"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"type":"object","required":["config"],"properties":{"name":{"type":"string","nullable":true,"description":"非空 name 优先；省略、null 或空白时回退到非空 config.metadata.name。"},"autoSuffixOnDuplicate":{"type":"boolean","default":true,"description":"名称重名处理策略。未传或为 true 时自动添加 (1)、(2) 等后缀；为 false 时返回名称重复错误"},"definitionType":{"type":"string","enum":["SCRIPT_JSON"],"default":"SCRIPT_JSON","description":"旧输入兼容字段，新调用方可省略。"},"config":{"description":"公共自动化 DSL；事件或定时触发。多条规则合并为同一个自动化对象；回读按执行语义规范化，不保证原分组或画布布局。","type":"object","required":["automations"],"properties":{"metadata":{"type":"object","description":"元数据结构，用于定义自动化的基本信息和变量。","properties":{"name":{"type":"string","description":"配置展示名；自动化创建可从此值回退。场景名必须由请求顶层 name 提供。"},"description":{"type":"string","description":"自动化的描述。"},"scope":{"type":"array","description":"定义设备的属性值变量，可在整个脚本中引用。","items":{"type":"object","description":"在 scope 中定义的设备属性变量。","required":["type","name","deviceId","endpointId","functionCode","traitCode"],"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","description":"值固定为 `device.property`。","enum":["device.property"]},"name":{"type":"string","description":"变量名称，在 scope 中定义时必须添加。"},"deviceId":{"type":"string","description":"设备 ID。"},"endpointId":{"type":"integer","description":"端点 ID。"},"functionCode":{"type":"string","description":"功能代码（如 OnOff、LevelControl）。"},"traitCode":{"type":"string","description":"功能点代码（如 OnOff、CurrentLevel）。"},"valuePath":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"default":"$","description":"结构体叶子的规范单值路径；标量和根值使用 $。"}},"additionalProperties":false,"title":"AutomationDslScopeDeviceProperty"}},"variables":{"type":"array","description":"定义 Flow 局部变量，可被 variable.flow 引用。每项的 value
